const personalTopN = computed(() => personalView.value.topN)\n343: const personalConfidenceLabel = computed(() => {\n344:   const c = personalView.value.confidence\n345:   if (c == null) return || 1))\n354: const introPassIndex = computed(() => Number(displayGroup.value?.pass_index || passIndex.value))\n355: \n356: const groupDoneCount = computed(() => (showGroupIntro.value ? 0 : pendingGroup.value.estimated_comparisons || 0)\n360:   }\n361:   return runner.inGroupTotal.value || 0\n362: })\n363: const groupDoneLabel = computed(() => `${groupDoneCount.value} / = Number(progress.value.min_expected_passes || 0)\n376:   const completed = Number(progress.value.passes_completed || 0)\n377:   if (minPasses > 0 && completed >= minPasses) return true\n378:   return minTarget.value > 0 && comparisonsDone.value >= minTarget.value\n379: })\n380: const overallPct = computed(() => {\n381:   if (pastMinTarget.value) return 100\n382:   const total = minTarget.value\n383:   if (!total) return 0\n384:   return Math.min(100, Math.round((100 * comparisonsDone.value) / total))\n385: })\n386: \n387: function reachedMaxRecommended(res: any): boolean {\n388:   if (maxCompleteAcknowledged.value) return false\n389:   const p = res.progress || {}\n390:   const maxP = Number(p.max_recommended_passes || projectSettings.value.max_recommended_passes || 0)\n391:   const completed = Number(p.passes_completed || 0)\n392:   if (!maxP || completed < maxP) return false\n393:   const g = res.next_group\n394:   if (!g) return true\n395:   return = 0\n402:   if (multiFactorMode.value) {\n403:     pendingGroup.value = g\n404:     showGroupIntro.value = true\n405:     runner.stop()\n406:   } else {\n407:     pendingGroup.value = null\n408:     showGroupIntro.value = false\n409:     runner.startGroup(g)\n410:   }\n411: }\n412: \n413: function beginPendingGroup() {\n414:   const g = pendingGroup.value\n415:   if (!g) return\n416:   showGroupIntro.value = false\n417:   pendingGroup.value = null\n418:   runner.startGroup(g)\n419: }\n420: \n421: function applyPayload(res: any) {\n422:   if (res.failure_reason) {\n423:     error.value = String(res.failure_reason)\n424:     return\n425:   }\n426:   error.value = ''\n427:   if (res.project) {\n428:     projectTitle.value = res.project.project_title || res.project.project_tag || 'Project'\n429:     if (res.project.factor_count != null) factorCount.value = Number(res.project.factor_count)\n430:     projectEndTime.value = res.project.end_time || null\n431:     projectDisabled.value = !!res.project.disabled\n432:     projectImageSrc.value = (res.project.has_project_branding_image && shareSession.unlocked.value)\n433:       ? brandingApi.shareProjectImageUrl(token.value, Date.now())\n434:       : ''\n435:   }\n436:   if (Array.isArray(res.factors)) factorCount.value = res.factors.length\n437:   progress.value = res.progress || {}\n438:   ranking.seedFromProgress(progress.value)\n439:   sessionComplete.value = Boolean(res.session_complete)\n440:   ranking.setCatalog(\n441:     (res.alternatives || []).map((a: any) => Number(a.id)).filter(Boolean),\n442:     (res.factors || []).map((f: any) => Number(f.id)).filter(Boolean),\n443:   )\n444:   projectSettings.value = res.project_settings || {}\n445:   const g = res.next_group as SortGroupPayload | null | undefined\n446: \n447:   if (reachedMaxRecommended(res)) {\n448:     showGroupIntro.value = false\n449:     pendingGroup.value = null\n450:     runner.stop()\n451:     heldNextGroup.value = g || null\n452:     maxCompleteView.value = true\n453:     return\n454:   }\n455: \n456:   maxCompleteView.value = false\n457:   if (g) beginGroup(g)\n458:   else {\n459:     pendingGroup.value = null\n460:     showGroupIntro.value = false\n461:     runner.stop()\n462:   }\n463: }\n464: \n465: function storeCredentials(form: Record<string, unknown>) {\n466:   gateCreds.value = mergeShareGateCreds(gateCreds.value, form, routeMagicKey.value)\n467: }\n468: \n469: async function access(form: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {\n470:   gateLoading.value = true\n471:   storeCredentials(form)\n472:   try {\n473:     const res = await shares.extAccess(token.value, shareKind.value, {\n474:       ...gateCreds.value,\n475:       request_next: true,\n476:       share_kind: shareKind.value,\n477:     }) as any\n478:     if (res.failure_reason && !res.project) {\n479:       denial.value = res\n480:       const alert = }\n485:     unlocked.value = true\n486:     shareSession.markUnlocked(token.value)\n487:     denial.value = null\n488:     gateMessage.value = ''\n489:     loading.value = true\n490:     applyPayload(res)\n491:     if (res.personal_vote?.is_complete && shareKind.value === 'vote_view') {\n492:       completeView.value = true\n493:       votesCaptured.value = Number(res.votes_captured || res.personal_vote?.comparison_count || 0)\n494:       submitterSummary.value = res.submitter_summary || null\n495:       showGroupIntro.value = false\n496:       pendingGroup.value = null\n497:       heldNextGroup.value = null\n498:       maxCompleteView.value = false\n499:       runner.stop()\n500:     }\n501:   } catch (e: any) {\n502:     gateMessage.value = e?.message || 'Access failed'\n503:     gateMessageType.value = 'error'\n504:   } finally {\n505:     gateLoading.value = false\n506:     loading.value = false\n507:   }\n508: }\n509: \n510: async function submitCompletedGroup() {\n511:   const pkg = runner.buildPackage()\n512:   if (!pkg) return\n513:   fetching.value = true\n514:   try {\n515:     const res = await shares.extCompleteGroup(token.value, {\n516:       ...gateCreds.value,\n517:       share_kind: shareKind.value,\n518:       prior_group: pkg,\n519:     }) as any\n520:     applyPayload(res)\n521:   } catch (e: any) {\n522: null\n537: \n538: function flushThresholds() {\n539:   return {\n540:     count: Math.max(1, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_flush_count || 5)),\n541:     idleMs: Math.max(5, const pkg = runner.buildPartialPackage()\n548:   if (!pkg || !pkg.pairings.length) return\n549:   if (pkg.pairings.length === lastFlushedCount.value) return\n550:   try {\n551:     await pkg.pairings.length\n557:   } catch { /* best-effort */ }\n558: }\n559: \n560: watch(\n561:   () => [runner.group.value, runner.pairings.value, runner.inGroupDone.value] as const,\n562:   () =>    if (!n || runner.complete.value) return\n570:     const { count, idleMs } = flushThresholds()\n571:     if (n - lastFlushedCount.value >= count) void flushPartial()\n572:     idleTimer =    </div>\n          <v-row v-if=\"personalOptions.length\" class=\"mt-2\">\n            <v-col cols=\"12\" :lg=\"showPersonalFactors ? 7 : 12\">\n              <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n                <v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n                  <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold mb-1\">Your ranking</div>\n                  <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-4\">\n                    Decision factors contribute according to their ranked importance; Rank percentage is first vs last likelihood.\n                  </div>\n                  <div v-for=\"(item, index) in                 <div class=\"flex-grow-1\">\n                        <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between ga-3\">\n                          <strong>{{ item.title }}</strong>\n                          <span class=\"font-weight-bold\">{{ formatPercent(item.score) }}</span>\n                        </div>\n                        <v-progress-linear :model-value=\"scoreBarPercent(item.score)\" </template>\n                            </template>\n                          </RankRangeBar>\n                          <template v-if=\"optionChanceCaption(item, personalRankingMode,       </div>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                </v-card-text>\n              </v-card>\n            </v-col>\n            <v-col v-if=\"showPersonalFactors\" cols=\"12\" lg=\"5\">\n              <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n                <v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n                  <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold mb-1\">What matters  :model-value=\"scoreBarPercent(criterion.normalizedWeight ?? criterion.weight ?? criterion.score ?? 0)\"\n                          height=\"8\"\n                          rounded\n                          color=\"deep-purple\"\n                          class=\"mt-2\"\n                        />\n                        <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-4 text-caption text-medium-emphasis      <template v-if=\"formatWeightCi(criterion.weight_ci95)\">\n                              ({{ formatWeightCi(criterion.weight_ci95) }})\n                            </template>\n                          </span>\n                          <span>Differentiation {{ formatNullablePercent(criterion.discrimination) }}</span>\n                          <span>Decision leverage {{ </v-card-text>\n              </v-card>\n            </v-col>\n          </v-row>\n        </template>\n\n        <template v-else>\n          <RankingConfidenceBar      <v-btn color=\"primary\" class=\"mt-4\" @click=\"onResume\">Continue</v-btn>\n          </div>\n\n          <template v-else>\n          <v-row class=\"mb-4\" dense>\n            <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n              <v-card class=\"soft-card\" rounded=\"lg\">\n                <v-card-text class=\"py-3\">\n                  <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between text-caption mb-2\">\n                    <span>This group</span>\n                    <strong>{{ groupDoneLabel }}</strong>\n                  </div>\n                  <v-progress-linear :model-value=\"inGroupPct\" height=\"9\" rounded color=\"primary\" />\n                </v-card-text>\n              </v-card>\n            </v-col>\n            <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n              <v-card class=\"soft-card\" rounded=\"lg\">\n                <v-card-text class=\"py-3\">\n                  <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between text-caption mb-2\">\n                    <span>Overall</span>\n                    <strong v-if=\"pastMinTarget\">Adding Optional Comparisons</strong>\n                  </div>\n                  <v-progress-linear v-if=\"fetching\" class=\"probe-stage question-group-loading\">\n            <div class=\"question-group-timer\" />\n            <div class=\"text-body-2\">Preparing next group…</div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div v-else-if=\"maxCompleteView\" class=\"empty-state py-12\">\n            <i class=\"fa-solid fa-flag-checkered fa-3x mb-3\" style=\"color:var(--pc-success)\" />\n            <div class=\"text-h5 font-weight-bold mb-2\">Maximum comparisons completed</div>\n            <div class=\"text-body-1 text-medium-emphasis mb-4\">\n              You've completed the maximum comparisons for this project.\n            </div>\n            <v-btn color=\"primary\" variant=\"flat\" class=\"mr-2\" :loading=\"submitting\" @click=\"markComplete\">Mark complete</v-btn>\n            <v-btn\n              v-if=\"heldNextGroup || !sessionComplete\"\n              variant=\"tonal\"\n              @click=\"continuePastMax\"\n            >\n              Continue comparing\n            </v-btn>\n          </div>\n\n          <div v-else-if=\"sessionComplete && !runner.group.value && !pendingGroup\" class=\"empty-state py-12\">\n            <div class=\"text-h6 v-else-if=\"showGroupIntro && displayGroup\" class=\"probe-stage\" :class=\"{ 'criteria-mode': introIsCriteria }\">\n            <CompareQuestionHeader\n              :is-criteria=\"introIsCriteria\"\n              :pass-index=\"introPassIndex\"\n              :criterion=\"displayGroup?.criterion\"\n              :criterion-id=\"displayGroup?.criterion_id\"\n              variant=\"intro\"\n            />\n            <div class=\"d-flex justify-center mt-6\">\n              <v-btn color=\"primary\" variant=\"flat\" size=\"large\" @click=\"beginPendingGroup\">\n                {{ introIsCriteria ? 'Compare factors' : 'Compare based on this factor' }}\n              </v-btn>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div v-else-if=\"runner.left.value && runner.right.value\" class=\"probe-stage\">\n            <CompareQuestionHeader\n              :is-criteria=\"isCriteria\"\n              :pass-index=\"passIndex\"\n              :criterion=\"runner.group.value?.criterion\"\n              :criterion-id=\"runner.group.value?.criterion_id\"\n              variant=\"pair\"\n            />\n            <v-row>\n              <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n                <div class=\"choice-card\" @click=\"runner.answer('winner', runner.left.value!.id)\">\n                  <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold\">{{ runner.left.value.title }}</div>\n                  <div v-if=\"runner.left.value.description\" class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis mt-2\">{{ runner.left.value.description }}</div>\n                </div>\n              </v-col>\n              <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n                <div class=\"choice-card\" @click=\"runner.answer('winner', runner.right.value!.id)\">\n                  <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold\">{{ runner.right.value.title }}</div>\n                  <div v-if=\"runner.right.value.description\" class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis mt-2\">{{ runner.right.value.description }}</div>\n                </div>\n              </v-col>\n            </v-row>\n            <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2 justify-center mt-4\">\n              <v-btn variant=\"text\" :disabled=\"!runner.canUndo.value\" @click=\"runner.undo()\">\n                <i class=\"fa-solid fa-rotate-left mr-2\" /> Undo\n              </v-btn>\n              <v-btn variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"runner.answer('tie')\">About equal</v-btn>\n              <v-btn variant=\"text\" @click=\"runner.answer('unsure')\">Not sure</v-btn>\n              <v-btn variant=\"text\" @click=\"runner.answer('skipped')\">Skip</v-btn>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div v-else class=\"probe-stage question-group-loading\">\n            <div class=\"question-group-timer\" />\n            <div class=\"text-body-2\">Sorting…</div>\n          </div>\n          </template>\n        </template>\n      </template>\n    </template>\n  </div>\n</template>\n\n<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { ShareAccessPayload } from '~/types/api'\nimport type { SortGroupPayload } from '~/composables/useSortGroupRunner'\nimport { collectionEndingSoon, collectionIsClosed, projectRemainingTimeCopy } from '~/utils/projectEndTime'\nimport { mergeShareGateCreds, shareGateDenialAlert } from '~/utils/shareGateCreds'\nimport { shareMagicKeyFromRoute } from '~/utils/shareRoute'\nimport {\n  formatPercent,\n  formatNullablePercent,\n  formatRankCi,\n  formatWeightCi,\n  formatExpectedRank,\n  scoreBarPercent,\n  optionChanceCaption,\n  personalRankingsFromSummary,\n  metricLabel,\n} from '~/utils/ranking'\n\nconst props = withDefaults(defineProps<{\n  shareKind?: 'vote' | 'vote_view'\n}>(), {\n  shareKind: 'vote',\n})\n\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst shares = useSharesApi()\nconst brandingApi = useBrandingApi()\nconst shareSession = useShareSession()\nconst runner = useSortGroupRunner()\nconst ranking = useRankingConfidence()\n\nconst token = computed(() => String(route.params.token || ''))\nconst routeMagicKey = computed(() => shareMagicKeyFromRoute(route))\nconst shareKind = computed(() => props.shareKind)\n\nconst unlocked = ref(false)\nconst gateCreds = ref<Record<string, unknown>>({})\nconst gateLoading = ref(false)\nconst denial = ref<ShareAccessPayload | null>(null)\nconst gateMessage = ref('')\nconst gateMessageType = ref<'error' | 'info' | 'success' | 'warning'>('info')\nconst loading = ref(false)\nconst fetching = ref(false)\nconst submitting = ref(false)\nconst error = ref('')\nconst projectTitle = ref('Project')\nconst projectImageSrc = ref('')\nconst projectEndTime = ref<string | null>(null)\nconst projectDisabled = ref(false)\nconst remainingTimeCopy = computed(() => projectRemainingTimeCopy(projectEndTime.value))\nconst collectionClosed = computed(() => collectionIsClosed(projectEndTime.value))\nconst endingSoon = computed(() => collectionEndingSoon(projectEndTime.value))\nconst inputClosed = computed(() => projectDisabled.value || collectionClosed.value)\nconst progress = ref<Record<string, unknown>>({})\nconst sessionComplete = ref(false)\nconst completeView = ref(false)\nconst paused = ref(false)\nconst votesCaptured = ref(0)\nconst submitterSummary = ref<Record<string, any> | null>(null)\nconst factorCount = ref(0)\nconst projectSettings = ref<Record<string, unknown>>({})\nconst pendingGroup = ref<SortGroupPayload | null>(null)\nconst showGroupIntro = ref(false)\nconst maxCompleteView = ref(false)\nconst maxCompleteAcknowledged = ref(false)\nconst heldNextGroup = ref<SortGroupPayload | null>(null)\n\nconst personalView = computed(() =>\n  personalRankingsFromSummary(submitterSummary.value, projectSettings.value),\n)\nconst personalOptions = computed(() => personalView.value.options)\nconst personalFactors = computed(() => personalView.value.factors)\nconst showPersonalFactors = computed(() => personalView.value.showFactors)\nconst personalRankingMode = computed(() => personalView.value.rankingMode)\nconst personalTopN = computed(() => personalView.value.topN)\nconst personalConfidenceLabel = computed(() => {\n  const c = personalView.value.confidence\n  if (c == null) return ''\n  return metricLabel(c)\n})\n\nconst multiFactorMode = computed(() => factorCount.value >= 2)\nconst displayGroup = computed(() => pendingGroup.value || runner.group.value)\nconst isCriteria = computed(() => String(runner.group.value?.group_type || '') === 'criteria')\nconst introIsCriteria = computed(() => String(displayGroup.value?.group_type || '') === 'criteria')\nconst passIndex = computed(() => Number(progress.value.pass_index || runner.group.value?.pass_index || displayGroup.value?.pass_index || 1))\nconst introPassIndex = computed(() => Number(displayGroup.value?.pass_index || passIndex.value))\n\nconst groupDoneCount = computed(() => (showGroupIntro.value ? 0 : runner.inGroupDone.value))\nconst groupTotalCount = computed(() => {\n  if (showGroupIntro.value && pendingGroup.value) {\n    return Number(pendingGroup.value.question_budget || pendingGroup.value.estimated_comparisons || 0)\n  }\n  return runner.inGroupTotal.value || 0\n})\nconst groupDoneLabel = computed(() => `${groupDoneCount.value} / ${groupTotalCount.value || '—'}`)\nconst inGroupPct = computed(() => {\n  const t = groupTotalCount.value || 0\n  if (!t) return 0\n  return Math.min(100, Math.round((100 * groupDoneCount.value) / t))\n})\n\nconst comparisonsDone = computed(() => {\n  return Number(progress.value.participant_comparisons_done || 0) + (showGroupIntro.value ? 0 : runner.inGroupDone.value)\n})\nconst minTarget = computed(() => Number(progress.value.estimated_comparisons_at_min || 0))\nconst pastMinTarget = computed(() => {\n  const minPasses = Number(progress.value.min_expected_passes || 0)\n  const completed = Number(progress.value.passes_completed || 0)\n  if (minPasses > 0 && completed >= minPasses) return true\n  return minTarget.value > 0 && comparisonsDone.value >= minTarget.value\n})\nconst overallPct = computed(() => {\n  if (pastMinTarget.value) return 100\n  const total = minTarget.value\n  if (!total) return 0\n  return Math.min(100, Math.round((100 * comparisonsDone.value) / total))\n})\n\nfunction reachedMaxRecommended(res: any): boolean {\n  if (maxCompleteAcknowledged.value) return false\n  const p = res.progress || {}\n  const maxP = Number(p.max_recommended_passes || projectSettings.value.max_recommended_passes || 0)\n  const completed = Number(p.passes_completed || 0)\n  if (!maxP || completed < maxP) return false\n  const g = res.next_group\n  if (!g) return true\n  return Number(g.pass_index || 0) > maxP\n}\n\nfunction beginGroup(g: SortGroupPayload) {\n  heldNextGroup.value = null\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  lastFlushedCount.value = 0\n  if (multiFactorMode.value) {\n    pendingGroup.value = g\n    showGroupIntro.value = true\n    runner.stop()\n  } else {\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    runner.startGroup(g)\n  }\n}\n\nfunction beginPendingGroup() {\n  const g = pendingGroup.value\n  if (!g) return\n  showGroupIntro.value = false\n  pendingGroup.value = null\n  runner.startGroup(g)\n}\n\nfunction applyPayload(res: any) {\n  if (res.failure_reason) {\n    error.value = String(res.failure_reason)\n    return\n  }\n  error.value = ''\n  if (res.project) {\n    projectTitle.value = res.project.project_title || res.project.project_tag || 'Project'\n    if (res.project.factor_count != null) factorCount.value = Number(res.project.factor_count)\n    projectEndTime.value = res.project.end_time || null\n    projectDisabled.value = !!res.project.disabled\n    projectImageSrc.value = (res.project.has_project_branding_image && shareSession.unlocked.value)\n      ? brandingApi.shareProjectImageUrl(token.value, Date.now())\n      : ''\n  }\n  if (Array.isArray(res.factors)) factorCount.value = res.factors.length\n  progress.value = res.progress || {}\n  ranking.seedFromProgress(progress.value)\n  sessionComplete.value = Boolean(res.session_complete)\n  ranking.setCatalog(\n    (res.alternatives || []).map((a: any) => Number(a.id)).filter(Boolean),\n    (res.factors || []).map((f: any) => Number(f.id)).filter(Boolean),\n  )\n  projectSettings.value = res.project_settings || {}\n  const g = res.next_group as SortGroupPayload | null | undefined\n\n  if (reachedMaxRecommended(res)) {\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    runner.stop()\n    heldNextGroup.value = g || null\n    maxCompleteView.value = true\n    return\n  }\n\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  if (g) beginGroup(g)\n  else {\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    runner.stop()\n  }\n}\n\nfunction storeCredentials(form: Record<string, unknown>) {\n  gateCreds.value = mergeShareGateCreds(gateCreds.value, form, routeMagicKey.value)\n}\n\nasync function access(form: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {\n  gateLoading.value = true\n  storeCredentials(form)\n  try {\n    const res = await shares.extAccess(token.value, shareKind.value, {\n      ...gateCreds.value,\n      request_next: true,\n      share_kind: shareKind.value,\n    }) as any\n    if (res.failure_reason && !res.project) {\n      denial.value = res\n      const alert = shareGateDenialAlert(res.failure_reason, !!res.sent_magic_access_message)\n      gateMessage.value = alert.message\n      gateMessageType.value = alert.type\n      return\n    }\n    unlocked.value = true\n    shareSession.markUnlocked(token.value)\n    denial.value = null\n    gateMessage.value = ''\n    loading.value = true\n    applyPayload(res)\n    if (res.personal_vote?.is_complete && shareKind.value === 'vote_view') {\n      completeView.value = true\n      votesCaptured.value = Number(res.votes_captured || res.personal_vote?.comparison_count || 0)\n      submitterSummary.value = res.submitter_summary || null\n      showGroupIntro.value = false\n      pendingGroup.value = null\n      heldNextGroup.value = null\n      maxCompleteView.value = false\n      runner.stop()\n    }\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    gateMessage.value = e?.message || 'Access failed'\n    gateMessageType.value = 'error'\n  } finally heldNextGroup.value = null\n  if (held) {\n    beginGroup(held)\n    return\n  }\n  fetching.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await shares.extNextGroup(token.value, {\n  return runner.inGroupTotal.value || 0\n236: })\n237: const groupDoneLabel = computed(() => `${groupDoneCount.value} / ${groupTotalCount.value || '—'}`)\n238: const inGroupPct = computed(() => {\n239: try {\n382:     await votes.saveGroup(projectId.value, pkg)\n383:     lastFlushedCount.value = pkg.pairings.length\n384:   } catch { /* best-effort */ }\n385: }\n386: \n387: watch(\n388:   () => else if (e.key === 'u' || e.key === 'U') onUndo()\n481: }\n482: \n483: onMounted(() => {\n484:   window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)\n485:   bootstrap()\n486: })\n487: onBeforeUnmount(() => </div>\n        </template>\n      </template>\n    </template>\n  </div>\n</template>\n\n<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { SortGroupPayload } from '~/composables/useSortGroupRunner'\nimport { collectionEndingSoon, collectionIsClosed, projectRemainingTimeCopy } from '~/utils/projectEndTime'\n\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst projectId = computed(() => Number(route.params.id))\nconst votes = useVotesApi()\nconst { loadProject, project, alternatives, factors } = useProjectWorkspace()\nconst runner = useSortGroupRunner()\nconst ranking = useRankingConfidence()\nconst remainingTimeCopy = computed(() => projectRemainingTimeCopy(project.value?.end_time))\nconst collectionClosed = computed(() => collectionIsClosed(project.value?.end_time))\nconst endingSoon = computed(() => collectionEndingSoon(project.value?.end_time))\nconst inputClosed = computed(() => !!project.value?.disabled || collectionClosed.value)\n\nconst loading = ref(true)\nconst fetching = ref(false)\nconst submitting = ref(false)\nconst error = ref('')\nconst completeView = ref(false)\nconst paused = ref(false)\nconst votesCaptured = ref(0)\nconst progress = ref<Record<string, unknown>>({})\nconst sessionComplete = ref(false)\nconst projectSettings = ref<Record<string, unknown>>({})\nconst pendingGroup = ref<SortGroupPayload | null>(null)\nconst showGroupIntro = ref(false)\nconst maxCompleteView = ref(false)\nconst maxCompleteAcknowledged = ref(false)\nconst heldNextGroup = ref<SortGroupPayload | null>(null)\n\nconst multiFactorMode = computed(() => {\n  const n = (factors.value || []).filter((f: any) => !f.disabled).length\n  return n >= 2\n})\n\nconst displayGroup = computed(() => pendingGroup.value || runner.group.value)\nconst isCriteria = computed(() => String(runner.group.value?.group_type || '') === 'criteria')\nconst introIsCriteria = computed(() => String(displayGroup.value?.group_type || '') === 'criteria')\nconst passIndex = computed(() => Number(progress.value.pass_index || runner.group.value?.pass_index || 1))\nconst introPassIndex = computed(() => Number(displayGroup.value?.pass_index || passIndex.value))\n\nconst groupDoneCount = computed(() => (showGroupIntro.value ? 0 : runner.inGroupDone.value))\nconst groupTotalCount = computed(() => {\n  if (showGroupIntro.value && pendingGroup.value) {\n    return Number(pendingGroup.value.question_budget || pendingGroup.value.estimated_comparisons || 0)\n  }\n  return runner.inGroupTotal.value || 0\n})\nconst groupDoneLabel = computed(() => `${groupDoneCount.value} / ${groupTotalCount.value || '—'}`)\nconst inGroupPct = computed(() => {\n  const t = groupTotalCount.value || 0\n  if (!t) return 0\n  return Math.min(100, Math.round((100 * groupDoneCount.value) / t))\n})\n\nconst comparisonsDone = computed(() => {\n  return Number(progress.value.participant_comparisons_done || 0) + (showGroupIntro.value ? 0 : runner.inGroupDone.value)\n})\nconst minTarget = computed(() => Number(progress.value.estimated_comparisons_at_min || 0))\nconst pastMinTarget = computed(() => {\n  const minPasses = Number(progress.value.min_expected_passes || 0)\n  const completed = Number(progress.value.passes_completed || 0)\n  if (minPasses > 0 && completed >= minPasses) return true\n  return minTarget.value > 0 && comparisonsDone.value >= minTarget.value\n})\nconst overallPct = computed(() => {\n  if (pastMinTarget.value) return 100\n  const total = minTarget.value\n  if (!total) return 0\n  return Math.min(100, Math.round((100 * comparisonsDone.value) / total))\n})\n\nfunction reachedMaxRecommended(res: Record<string, unknown>): boolean {\n  if (maxCompleteAcknowledged.value) return false\n  const p = (res.progress as Record<string, unknown>) || {}\n  const maxP = Number(p.max_recommended_passes || projectSettings.value.max_recommended_passes || 0)\n  const completed = Number(p.passes_completed || 0)\n  if (!maxP || completed < maxP) return false\n  const g = res.next_group as SortGroupPayload | null | undefined\n  if (!g) return true\n  return Number(g.pass_index || 0) > maxP\n}\n\nfunction beginGroup(g: SortGroupPayload) {\n  heldNextGroup.value = null\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  lastFlushedCount.value = 0\n  if (multiFactorMode.value) {\n    pendingGroup.value = g\n    showGroupIntro.value = true\n    runner.stop()\n  } else {\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    runner.startGroup(g)\n  }\n}\n\nfunction beginPendingGroup() {\n  const g = pendingGroup.value\n  if (!g) return\n  showGroupIntro.value = false\n  pendingGroup.value = null\n  runner.startGroup(g)\n}\n\nasync function applySession(res: Record<string, unknown>) {\n  if (res.failure_reason) {\n    error.value = String(res.failure_reason)\n    return\n  }\n  error.value = ''\n  progress.value = (res.progress as Record<string, unknown>) || {}\n  ranking.seedFromProgress(progress.value)\n  sessionComplete.value = Boolean(res.session_complete)\n  projectSettings.value = (res.project_settings as Record<string, unknown>) || {}\n  const g = res.next_group as SortGroupPayload | null | undefined\n\n  if (reachedMaxRecommended(res)) {\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    runner.stop()\n    heldNextGroup.value = g || null\n    maxCompleteView.value = true\n    return\n  }\n\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  if (g) {\n    beginGroup(g)\n  } else {\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    runner.stop()\n  }\n}\n\nasync function bootstrap() {\n  loading.value = true\n  try {\n    await loadProject(projectId.value, { myVote: false })\n    ranking.setCatalog(\n      (alternatives.value || []).filter((a: any) => !a.disabled).map((a: any) => Number(a.id)),\n      (factors.value || []).filter((f: any) => !f.disabled).map((f: any) => Number(f.id)),\n    )\n    const res = await votes.getMyVote(projectId.value, true) as any\n    await applySession(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to load compare session'\n  } finally {\n    loading.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function submitCompletedGroup() {\n  const pkg = runner.buildPackage()\n  if (!pkg) return\n  fetching.value = true\n  submitting.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votes.completeGroup(projectId.value, pkg) as any\n    await applySession(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to save group'\n  } finally {\n    fetching.value = false\n    submitting.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nwatch(\n  () => runner.complete.value,\n  async (done) => {\n    if (done && runner.group.value) await submitCompletedGroup()\n  },\n)\n\nconst lastFlushedCount = ref(0)\nlet idleTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null\n\nfunction flushThresholds() {\n  return {\n    count: Math.max(1, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_flush_count || 5)),\n    idleMs: Math.max(5, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_idle_seconds || 90)) * 1000,\n  }\n}\n\nasync function flushPartial() {\n  if (runner.complete.value || submitting.value) return\n  const pkg = runner.buildPartialPackage()\n  if (!pkg || !pkg.pairings.length) return\n  if (pkg.pairings.length === lastFlushedCount.value) return\n  try {\n    await votes.saveGroup(projectId.value, pkg)\n    lastFlushedCount.value = pkg.pairings.length\n  } catch { /* best-effort */ }\n}\n\nwatch(\n  () => [runner.group.value, runner.pairings.value, runner.inGroupDone.value] as const,\n  () => ranking.setLive(runner.group.value, runner.pairings.value),\n)\n\nwatch(\n  () => runner.inGroupDone.value,\n  (n) => {\n    if (idleTimer) clearTimeout(idleTimer)\n    if (!n || runner.complete.value) return\n    const { count, idleMs } = flushThresholds()\n    if (n - lastFlushedCount.value >= count) void flushPartial()\n    idleTimer = setTimeout(() => { void flushPartial() }, idleMs)\n  },\n)\n\nonBeforeUnmount(() => {\n  if (idleTimer) clearTimeout(idleTimer)\n})\n\nfunction onUndo() {\n  runner.undo()\n}\n\nfunction onPause() {\n  paused.value = true\n  runner.setPaused(true)\n}\n\nfunction onResume() {\n  paused.value = false\n  runner.setPaused(false)\n}\n\nasync function markComplete() {\n  submitting.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votes.markComplete(projectId.value, true) as any\n    votesCaptured.value = Number(res.votes_captured || 0)\n    completeView.value = true\n    maxCompleteView.value = false\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    heldNextGroup.value = null\n    runner.stop()\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to mark complete'\n  } finally {\n    submitting.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function continuePastMax() {\n  maxCompleteAcknowledged.value = true\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  const held = heldNextGroup.value\n  heldNextGroup.value = null\n  if (held) {\n    beginGroup(held)\n    return\n  }\n  fetching.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votes.nextGroup(projectId.value) as any\n    await applySession(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to continue comparisons'\n  } finally {\n    fetching.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function resumeAfterComplete() {\n  fetching.value = true\n  try {\n    await votes.markComplete(projectId.value, false)\n    completeView.value = false\n    maxCompleteAcknowledged.value = true\n    const res = await votes.nextGroup(projectId.value) as any\n    await applySession(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to resume comparisons'\n  } finally {\n    fetching.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nfunction onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {\n  if (completeView.value || paused.value || maxCompleteView.value || showGroupIntro.value || !runner.left.value) return\n  if (e.key === '1') runner.answer('winner', runner.left.value.id)\n  else if (e.key === '2') runner.answer('winner', runner.right.value!.id)\n  else if (e.key === 'e' || e.key === 'E') runner.answer('tie')\n  else if (e.key === 'n' || e.key === 'N') runner.answer('unsure')\n  else if (e.key === 'u' || e.key === 'U') onUndo()\n}\n\nonMounted(() => {\n  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)\n  bootstrap()\n})\nonBeforeUnmount(() => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey))\n</script>","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":488,"totalLines":488,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/pages/projects/[id]/probe.vue","time":{"start":1787342014349,"end":1787342014478}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a025e2c4c47a73a6dbdd79842f70a8"}},"id":"prt_025e2c509001p6z7JFoITrxIF3","sessionID":"ses_fda1eb328ffeXm2wfd5c25eHEt","messageID":"msg_025e26c2e0015ir8tMMHl1x9qS"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787342103708,"sessionID":"ses_fda1eb328ffeXm2wfd5c25eHEt","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a025e41fdf7451b8260f9d62778173","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/config/config_settings.py"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/config/config_settings.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: from os import environ\n2: \n3: from ..pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfGlobalSettings\n4: \n5: class GlobalSettings(PvfGlobalSettings):\n6:     \"\"\"PowerChoice application settings.\n7: \n8:     Framework-generic values (share links, external API, watcher, email, stripe,\n9:     tokens) live in PvfGlobalSettings. {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787342109406,"sessionID":"ses_fda1eb328ffeXm2wfd5c25eHEt","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a025e43737763188494e7990226ee7","state":{"status":"error","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/CHANGELOG.md"},"error":"File not found: /workspaces/powerchoice/CHANGELOG.md","time":{"start":1787342109335,"end":1787342109395}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a025e43737763188494e7990226ee7"}},"id":"prt_025e43778001TIQLYoDO0K6Hk0","sessionID":"ses_fda1eb328ffeXm2wfd5c25eHEt","messageID":"msg_025e42918001AZ1xJCzHba9CcN"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787342113591,"sessionID":"ses_fda1eb328ffeXm2wfd5c25eHEt","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"bash","callID":"call_01a025e445d27e739be3549b17a3c24f","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"command":"ls /workspaces/powerchoice/*.md 2>/dev/null; cat /workspaces/powerchoice/README.md | head -n 80"},"output":"/workspaces/powerchoice/AGENTS.md\n/workspaces/powerchoice/README.md\n# Power Choice Pro\n\nStructured decision support: teams define a **Project**, add **Options** and **Factors**, **Compare** them pairwise, and review **Results**. Share links collect participant input (Compare Only / Compare and See Only Your Results / See Full Results). User-facing language lives in [`docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md`](docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md).\n\nPython 3.13 + FastAPI + SQLModel + Alembic (Postgres). Nuxt 3 SPA in `client/` generates into `static_client/` (gitignored) and is served by FastAPI.\n\n## Pratt Ventures Framework (pvf) — keep isolated\n\nThe app runs on **pvf** (`src/pvf/`). pvf is a reusable multi-tenant kernel (session JWT, customer/user admin, email, share links, signed external APIs, Stripe, branding, logging). It may be managed or replaced as a separate tree. **Keep it isolated.**\n\nCurrent isolation patterns (do not erode these):\n\n- **pvf never imports this application.** The runner loads the shell named in `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` (`src.app_shell` / `app_startup`). Application code reaches pvf only through published entry points — see [`src/pvf/README.md`](src/pvf/README.md).\n- **Do not put Power Choice logic inside `src/pvf/`.** Product routes, models, ranking, and UI bindings belong in `src/api/`, `src/db/`, `src/utils/`, `src/app_shell.py`, and `client/`.\n- **Do not reach past published pvf surfaces** (`pvf_app_runner`, `pvf_get_alembic_config`, `pvf.utils.pvf_services`, `pvf.pvf_invocation`, `pvf.config.pvf_config_settings`, `pvf.depends.*`, `pvf.db.models.*`).\n- **Extend, don’t fork.** Extra columns on pvf tables go through `register_schema_extensions` in the shell. Feature switches and share vocabularies stay in `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` (deployment-fixed, read-only at runtime).\n- Framework tables live in `src/pvf/db/models/`; application tables live in `src/db/models/` and are registered from `src/db/models/bootstrap.py`.\n\n## Directory structure\n\n| Path | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `src/` | Backend. Poetry project (`package-mode = false`); in-project venv is `src/.venv`. |\n| `src/pvf/` | **Framework only.** Runner, four FastAPI apps, auth/share/external/Stripe/email, framework models. Treat as a separate product. |\n| `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` | Deployment-fixed pvf config: shell module, env file name, feature switches, share types. |\n| `src/app_shell.py` | Application bridge: settings merge, hooks, routers, schema extensions. Shared with the watcher. |\n| `src/api/` | Power Choice session/share/external routes (projects, options/factors, compare, results). |\n| `src/db/models/` | Application tables (projects, options/factors, vote events). Wired in `bootstrap.py`. |\n| `src/utils/` | Application services (ranking, compare session, templates, user context). |\n| `src/config/` | Application `GlobalSettings` (extends pvf settings) and client-facing settings. |\n| `src/alembic/` | Migrations. `env.py` is a thin shim over `pvf_get_alembic_config`. |\n| `src/powerchoice_watcher.py` | Separate process: signed webhook callback delivery. Not started by `./start-server.sh`. |\n| `src/powerchoice_server.py` | Compatibility shim that re-exports the pvf runner `app`. |\n| `src/tests/` | API tests against the configured DB (not hermetic). Helpers in `src/tests/helpers/`. |\n| `client/` | Nuxt 3 SPA (`ssr: false`) + Vue 3 + Vuetify. Dev on `:3000`, proxies API to `:8100`. |\n| `static_client/` | Generated SPA FastAPI serves. Gitignored. Rebuild with `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh`. |\n| `static/` | Non-SPA static files (`/static`). |\n| `scripts/` | Migrations, OpenAPI extract, Nuxt publish, Stripe CLI helpers. |\n| `e2e/` | Playwright. Starts/reuses API `:8100` + Nuxt `:3000`. |\n| `email_templates/` | SMTP-Jinja templates (html/txt/subject). |\n| `docs_examples/` | Product language, share-mode notes, prototypes — not runtime. |\n| `build/` | Docker image, supervisord, sample compose. |\n| `.devcontainer/` | Dev container + Postgres 16 sidecar. |\n| `openapi_sessions.json` / `openapi_api.json` | Contract snapshots for the session UI surface and customer toolkit API. |\n| `example.env` | Runtime env template. Copy to `.env` at the repo root (`*.env` is gitignored). |\n| `project_templates.yml` | Seed project / option / factor templates. |\n| `AGENTS.md` | Engineering conventions for this repo (commands, testing, OpenAPI). |\n\n## HTTP surfaces\n\nComposed by `src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py`, then handed to `src/app_shell.py`:\n\n| App | Mount | Auth | Role |\n|-----|-------|------|------|\n| root | `/` | — | Static + SPA fallback + Stripe hooks; `/status`, `/alive` |\n| noauth | `/auth-ws` | none | Login, password reset, self-registration |\n| session | `/ws` | JWT cookie | Session UI API |\n| share | `/ext-ws` | share cookie / magic key | Share-link compare and results |\n| external | `/api` | signed headers | Customer toolkit API |\n\nSession OpenAPI: `/session/docs`. Toolkit OpenAPI: `/api/docs`.\n\n## Local development\n\nDev container (recommended): reopen in the container, then:\n\n```bash\nmkdir -p /workspaces/powerchoice/postgres-data-${USER}   # before first compose start\ncp example.env .env                                       # set UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK=1 and secrets\ncd src && poetry install\ncd ..\n./scripts/migrations/run-migrations.sh\n./start-server.sh                                         # fastapi dev → 0.0.0.0:8100\n```\n\nUI in another terminal:\n\n```bash\ncd client && npm install && npm run dev                   # :3000, proxies to :8100\n```\n","metadata":{"output":"/workspaces/powerchoice/AGENTS.md\n/workspaces/powerchoice/README.md\n# Power Choice Pro\n\nStructured decision support: teams define a **Project**, add **Options** and **Factors**, **Compare** them pairwise, and review **Results**. Starts/reuses API `:8100` + Nuxt `:3000`. |\n| `email_templates/` | SMTP-Jinja templates (html/txt/subject). |\n| `docs_examples/` | Product language, share-mode notes, prototypes — not runtime. |\n| `build/` | Docker image, supervisord, sample compose. |\n| `.devcontainer/` | Dev container + Postgres 16 sidecar. |\n| `openapi_sessions.json` / `openapi_api.json` | Contract snapshots for the session UI surface and customer toolkit API. |\n| `example.env` | Runtime env template. Copy to `.env` at the repo root (`*.env` is gitignored). |\n| `project_templates.yml` | Seed project / option / factor templates. |\n| `AGENTS.md` | Engineering conventions for this repo (commands, testing, OpenAPI). |\n\n## HTTP surfaces\n\nComposed by `src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py`, then handed to `src/app_shell.py`:\n\n| App | Mount | Auth | Role |\n|-----|-------|------|------|\n| root | `/` | — | Static + SPA fallback + Stripe hooks; `/status`, `/alive` |\n| noauth | `/auth-ws` | none | Login, password reset, self-registration |\n| session | `/ws` | JWT cookie | Session UI API |\n| share | `/ext-ws` | share cookie / magic key | Share-link compare and results |\n| external | `/api` | signed headers | Customer toolkit API |\n\nSession OpenAPI: `/session/docs`. Toolkit OpenAPI: `/api/docs`.\n\n## Local development\n\nDev container (recommended): reopen in the container, then:\n\n```bash\nmkdir -p /workspaces/powerchoice/postgres-data-${USER}   # before first compose start\ncp example.env .env                                       # set UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK=1 and secrets\ncd src && poetry install\ncd ..\n./scripts/migrations/run-migrations.sh\n./start-server.sh                                         # fastapi dev → 0.0.0.0:8100\n```\n\nUI in another terminal:\n\n```bash\ncd client && npm install && npm run dev                   # :3000, proxies to :8100\n```\n","exit":0,"truncated":false},"title":"ls /workspaces/powerchoice/*.md 2>/dev/null; cat /workspaces/powerchoice/README.md | head -n 80","time":{"start":1787342113509,"end":1787342113527}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a025e445d27e739be3549b17a3c24f"}},"id":"prt_025e4462c001VxhVWBJpTFFqCw","sessionID":"ses_fda1eb328ffeXm2wfd5c25eHEt","messageID":"msg_025e43ecc001tb79cHbWbbx1O8"}}
